## Key Insights (Carry Forward)

> **#1-55 archived** to [memory/archive-insights-001-055.md](memory/archive-insights-001-055.md). Core themes: Memory ≠ RAG · Write-time filtering > retrieval ranking · Knowledge units > entities · Plugin-first distribution · Experience Compression Spectrum (L0-L3) · Skill contracts/versioning · Pareto frontier evaluation · Causal analytics pipeline · Phantom commit risk · Lean context > full context · Auditability-by-construction · Retrieval-side is 2026 frontier · Meta-adaptation > content adaptation · Per-skill memory = npm killer feature · Generative latent memory threat · Physics-inspired decay.
>
> **#57-128 archived** to [memory/archive-2026-07-mid.md](memory/archive-2026-07-mid.md). Thematic summary:
> - **Quality & Evaluation**: Gap detection + dual-loop + evaluation quartet = competitive moat (#57-65, #107)
> - **MCP & Tooling**: Zero graph MCP servers. 8-12 curated tools. Factory pattern. cacheHints 3-line ROI (#61-63, #81-90, #99, #104-106)
> - **TypeScript Moat**: npm ZERO TS-native graph memory libs. Graph > Filesystem (#66-70)
> - **Trust & Safety**: SimHash 0.974 AUC. Pre-execution gating > reputation (#112-115, #118)
> - **Entropy Family**: Degree 10 APIs / Spectral (von Neumann) / Centrality (AZI, edge-betweenness) / Generalized (Rényi + Tsallis) (#109-110, #116-117, #122)
> - **Inter-graph Trilogy**: JSD + CE + KL = complete information-theoretic suite (#123, #126)
> - **Spectral**: Von Neumann = global topology. Temporal trajectory = health metric. QJSD for graph comparison (#124-125)
> - **Forgetting & Retrieval**: Entropy-weighted forgetting + retrieval = TWO publishable contributions (#121, #127-128)
> - **Multi-Agent**: Coordination defects = 41-87% failures. Tree search = shared memory (#119-120)
> - **Entity Resolution**: Closes gap with Mem0/Graphiti (#127)
129. **Von Neumann spectral entropy captures global topology (c292, #031)** — Shannon entropy of normalized Laplacian eigenvalues. K_n maximizes H=ln(n-1) — opposite of degree-entropy where K_n=0. Well-connected graph = HIGH spectral entropy (healthy), fragmented = LOW (unhealthy). Existing _sym_eigenvalues infra → ~50 lines.
130. **Mem0 v3 ADD-only validates amg conflict resolution (#032)** — Mem0 dropped UPDATE/DELETE for 3x latency reduction + higher accuracy. amg's merge/consolidation approach was right all along. Graph-based resolution > vector dedup.
131. **Bi-temporal is now table stakes — Engram 83.6% vs 73.2% full-context (#033)** — Bi-temporal fact graphs (valid_time + transaction_time, invalidate-never-delete) beat full-context by +10.4pp on LongMemEval. amg already has bi-temporal edge tracking but doesn't expose `query_as_of(timestamp)` as first-class API. Graphiti's moat is no longer unique.
132. **Dual-process System-1/System-2 is THE production architecture pattern (#033)** — Every high-performing system (Engram, Mem0 v3, H-Mem) separates hot write path (no LLM, O(1) append) from cold async consolidation. amg's synchronous write path is fine for batch but wrong for real-time. FastAppendQueue needed.
133. **Benchmark harness IS the competitive weapon (#033)** — Same system appears as 58%/66%/92% across sources. Engram ships neutral reproducible harness with official judge. For amg npm launch: shipping benchmark harness creates ecosystem lock-in. Being the scoreboard = power.
134. **H-Mem hybrid tree+graph reveals amg's missing temporal abstraction layer (#033)** — H-Mem's temporal-semantic tree (daily→weekly→monthly summaries) is simpler version of amg's planned compress_to_skill (L0→L3). SummaryTree layer on top of graph would fill temporal abstraction gap.

135. **Spectral divergence = histogram-based inter-graph comparison, size-invariant (c308)** — Bin Laplacian eigenvalues into common [0,λ_max] grid, normalize to probability distributions, compute JSD/KL/CE. Unlike quantum_jensen_shannon_distance (elementwise, same-size only), histogram approach works for different-size graphs. 3 measures in one unified API. Complements degree-based trilogy with spectral-domain comparison.
136. **Multi-resolution scan reveals structural scale of divergence (c309)** — Fibonacci-like bins [2,3,5,8,13,21,34,55] sweep from coarse (global topology) to fine (individual eigenvalues). Peak resolution = where graphs differ most. Convergence (CV<0.05 last 3) = when enough resolution. Monotonicity direction = whether divergence grows or shrinks with resolution. Transforms single-number comparison into analytical instrument.

---

137. **Beta distribution gives confidence for free — Wilson LB prevents lucky-early-access attacks (#035)** — Beta(α,β) variance = confidence measure. Agent with 2✓0✗ has mean=1.0 but Wilson LB (95%) = 0.34. Using Wilson LB for critical-risk decisions means untested agents can't access dangerous capabilities despite perfect mean. Two-tier: mean for low/medium, Wilson LB for high/critical.
138. **A2A Agent Cards are the perfect trust anchor — signed identity + declared capabilities (#035)** — A2A Protocol v1.0.0 defines Agent Cards as signed JSON with publicKeyJwk, skills[], authentication schemes. This maps 1:1 to trust model: identity (who), capabilities (what they claim), verification (signature). Mismatch between declared skills and observed performance is itself a trust signal.
139. **Trust propagation needs hop decay — gossip without decay is poisonous (#035)** — EigenTrust-style distributed trust without hop decay lets a single compromised agent amplify trust through colluding chains. hopDecay=0.5/hop bounds influence to direct neighborhood. Combined with content fingerprinting (SimHash) that catches adversarial patterns behavioral metrics miss = defense in depth.

---

140. **Rényi entropy order α is graph resolution control (#036)** — Low α (→0) emphasizes graph size/connectivity. High α (→∞, min-entropy) detects bottlenecks and hubs. The CURVE of H(α) across orders is a structural fingerprint: flat=regular, steep=heterogeneous, convex with knee=mixed community+hub structure. A scan across α ∈ {0.5,1,2,3,5,8,20,∞} produces a unique signature for graph families. No npm/PyPI competitor has entropy scan capability.
141. **Entropy curve SHAPE (not scalar) is the graph fingerprint (#036)** — Single entropy value tells little. But a VECTOR of entropies at multiple scales — Shannon, Rényi(α=2,3,5,∞), Tsallis, von Neumann, edge-betweenness — creates a unique fingerprint. Two graphs with identical degree distributions but different clustering have identical Shannon degree entropy but diverge on spectral entropy. Combined degree+spectral fingerprint correctly classifies 6/7 graph families (verified in code). The shape (monotonic, range, convergence CV) is the analytical instrument.

---

142. **Three-layer router cascade = production-pattern MemFlow implemented (c317)** — Rules layer commits immediately for high-confidence intents (basic/temporal/constraint/global). Entropy layer escalates for ambiguous queries using graph topology. Fallback layer catches edge cases. Full cascade_trace with per-layer latency = production routing observability. First npm library with entropy-aware query routing.
143. **Spectral classification completes the identification stack (c318)** — Three methods in one API: spectral (single-resolution histogram JSD/KL/CE), spectral_scan (multi-resolution mean across Fibonacci bins), fingerprint (L2 on 12+ dim entropy feature vectors). Confidence = separation ratio between best and second-best match. Margin = absolute gap. Enables graph family identification without training data.
144. **Entropy fingerprint = compact graph DNA (c314)** — 12+ dimensional feature vector (Shannon degree, Rényi α=2/3/5/∞, Tsallis, von Neumann, edge-betweenness, etc). Two graphs with identical degree distributions but different clustering have identical Shannon degree entropy but diverge on spectral entropy. Combined degree+spectral fingerprint correctly classifies 6/7 graph families. Not a scalar — a vector signature.
145. **Classification with rejection = production safety layer (c320)** — classify→reject/accept pipeline. Dual criteria (score threshold + min margin) prevents low-confidence misclassification. Exact match always accepted (score≈0). Calibrated confidence linear rescale to [0,1]. Works with all 3 classification APIs. First npm library with entropy-aware classification rejection.
146. **The scoreboard IS the moat (#037)** — Shipping a neutral benchmark harness alongside amg makes amg's scores "official" (reference implementation). Competitors must either use amg's harness (endorsing it) or build their own (looking defensive). Mem0 already does this with Python-only memory-benchmarks. amg's harness should be TypeScript-native with clean MemoryBackend interface.
147. **Operation-level evaluation = differentiator no competitor has (#037)** — MemOps proves final-answer scoring conflates failure modes. amg already has lifecycle hooks (governance, entity resolution, forgetting, gap detection). Exposing as MemOps-compatible structured traces creates evaluation layer impossible without deep architectural integration. Moat within the moat.
148. **Scale tiers reveal non-linear failure modes (#037)** — BEAM: temporal reasoning 0.618 (1M) → 0.163 (10M). Event ordering 0.536 → 0.202. Memory systems have cliffs. amg's entropy-weighted retrieval + forgetting should theoretically prevent cliffs. Harness must include scale tiers.
149. **LongMemEval-V2 shifts to agentic multimodal context (May 2026, #037)** — 451 questions, up to 115M tokens, web trajectories with screenshots. 5 new ability types. "Environment gotchas" + "premise awareness" align with amg's knowledge gap detection + entity resolution.
150. **Fixed reader model is the reproducibility key (#037)** — LongMemEval-V2 uses Qwen3.5-9B as fixed reader. Eliminates "better reader = better score" variance. For amg-bench: default to small reproducible model (Qwen-9B/Llama-8B via Ollama) for answerer, stronger model for judge. Enables fully reproducible offline runs.

---

151. **RRF is the zero-parameter default for multi-method graph classification (#038)** — Reciprocal Rank Fusion (1/(k+rank)) from IR translates perfectly to graph classification. Scale-invariant (degree JSD ∈ [0,0.8], spectral ∈ [0,2.5], fingerprint ∈ [0,15+] — RRF ignores all scale differences). k=60 was optimized for web search (hundreds of results); for amg's 5-20 reference graphs, k=5-10 is more discriminative. No tuning needed — the rank-only approach aligns with amg's zero-dependency philosophy. In simulated tests: RRF correctly identifies graph_A when 2/3 methods rank it #1.
152. **Oracle diversity gain quantifies ensemble value (#038)** — Before building ensemble, measure: if ANY single method is correct per case, how much does the oracle upper bound exceed the best single method? Simulated results: degree=78%, spectral=78%, fingerprint=77%, but oracle=100% → 22% diversity gain. Double fault rates (degree-spectral: 2%, spectral-fingerprint: 4%, degree-fingerprint: 3%) confirm strong complementarity. If diversity gain < 5%, ensemble is not worth the complexity. Amg's degree-spectral pair has the lowest double fault (2%) — strongest complementarity pair.

153. **Classification suite complete: 9 APIs covering 5 paradigms (c319-330)** — Single-match (graph + spectral), ensemble (hybrid 2-modal + weighted_average 3-modal + rrf parameter-free + bayesian adaptive), rejection (threshold+margin safety layer), consensus (classification_compare multi-method report), and k-NN (distance-weighted voting with label pooling). Research #038 fully implemented. No npm/PyPI competitor has ANY graph classification, let alone 9 APIs across 5 paradigms. The classification pipeline is: classify (choose method) → reject/accept (threshold) → consensus (compare methods) → k-NN (robustness check).
154. **k-NN label pooling = category-level classification (c329)** — When multiple reference graphs share a category label (e.g., 3 star prototypes, 2 path prototypes), k-NN pools their votes. This is the first API that can answer "what FAMILY does this graph belong to?" rather than "which single reference is closest?". Distance-weighted (1/(|score|+ε)) ensures closer neighbors dominate. Combined with 5 base method backends, k-NN becomes a meta-classifier that improves any single-method result.
155. **Bayesian per-query adaptive weights = zero-parameter ensemble (c327)** — Separation metric (norm_second − norm_best)/(norm_max − norm_best) measures how decisively each method identifies the best reference. Methods that are ambiguous for one graph but decisive for another automatically get different influence. This is fundamentally different from fixed-weight ensembles: the weights adapt to each query's structural characteristics. No free parameters, no training data needed.

156. **Graph topology IS the intelligence — not just a storage format (#040)** — HAGE proves query-conditioned edges produce different retrieval paths for the same graph. HyphaeDB proves HNSW topology can serve as multi-agent communication fabric. The shift from "graph as storage" to "graph as reasoning instrument" validates amg's entropy framework — we extract intelligence FROM topology, not just FROM node content. Every competitor stores facts in graphs; amg reasons about graph structure itself.
157. **Dependency edges enable cascading memory correction (#040)** — GRADE shows agent traces need dependency edges ("A was inferred from B") to enable failure prediction and cascading invalidation. amg currently lacks provenance edges. Adding `kind="depends_on"` + `propagate_invalidation()` = 60-line API that no competitor has. When a base fact is corrected, all inferred facts are automatically marked stale.
158. **LLMs rubber-stamp GNN tool outputs 97.6-99.2% — tools must decide internally (#040)** — Stronger LLMs defer MORE, not less. Selective invocation must be designed in, not expected to emerge from scale. amg's `three_layer_router_cascade` already makes routing decisions before the LLM sees results — this is the correct pattern. Tools should make decisions, not offer suggestions for LLMs to rubber-stamp.
159. **HAGE's relation-specific views = multi-perspective retrieval (#040)** — Same graph, different edge projections depending on query intent. A temporal query projects to temporal edges; a causal query projects to causal edges. amg has relation-typed edges but traverses uniformly. `project_graph(relationType)` = 30-line API that enables relation-specific algorithms on subgraphs. HAGE adds RL-trained edge weights for making this automatic — future work when amg-bench provides reward signals.

160. **Classification benchmark reveals structural blind spots (#038, c334)** — Standardized evaluation with 6 canonical topology types (star/path/cycle/complete/bipartite/tree) shows most classification methods score ~20-33% on cross-topology identification. This is an honest, genuine insight: degree/spectral/fingerprint similarity metrics are good at distinguishing graphs within the same family but not across families. Path→star confusion dominates. The benchmark closes the build→evaluate→improve loop — without it, we'd be claiming superiority without evidence. Confusion matrix is the key analytical instrument.
161. **z_score is the scale-invariant confidence metric from rankings (#038, c335)** — `(best - mean_rest) / std_rest` computed from each method's rankings list. Unlike margin (absolute gap) or confidence ratio, z_score normalises across methods with fundamentally different score scales (degree JSD ∈ [0,0.8], spectral ∈ [0,2.5], fingerprint ∈ [0,15+]). More negative z_score = more confident. Zero-cost instrumentation from existing rankings data. Makes max_confidence_classification work correctly across heterogeneous methods — the meta-classifier can fairly compare degree vs spectral vs fingerprint confidence in a unified framework.
162. **Conviction vs consensus completes the classification meta-strategy space (#038, c335)** — classification_compare uses majority voting (consensus — trusts the crowd). max_confidence uses per-query best method (conviction — trusts the specialist). These are complementary, not competing: consensus works when methods collectively cover weaknesses; conviction works when one method is decisively correct for a specific query. Agreement fraction in max_confidence results provides the sanity check: a high-confidence-but-low-agreement result might warrant investigation. Together they span the meta-classification strategy space.

163. **Cascading invalidation is the unsolved problem — and the opportunity (#041)** — STALE (arXiv:2605.06527) is the first benchmark to systematically isolate Type II (cascading) invalidation: when an upstream attribute change logically invalidates a dependent belief without explicitly mentioning it. Every prior benchmark has × in the cascading column. Best model (Gemini-3.1-pro) only 55.2% overall, worse on cascading cases. For amg: `propagate_invalidation()` with explicit `depends_on` edges would be the **first npm library** with cascading correction capability. No competitor (Mem0, Zep, Letta, Cognee) has this.
164. **Write-side adjudication beats query-time cleverness by +13pp (#041)** — CUPMem resolves conflicts at write time, not query time. An LLM adjudicator decides KEEP/STALE/REPLACE/UNKNOWN for each new evidence against existing state. Topology-triggered search extends beyond directly-touched slots to structurally affected regions via dependency topology. This is architecturally identical to amg's `write_governance_check()` — but amg lacks the propagation step. The gap is precisely `propagate_invalidation()`.
165. **The attachment grade is the missing inductive bias for dependency edges (#041)** — GRADE (arXiv:2606.22741) proves that observed dependency edges carry transferable failure-prediction signal, while inferred (full-history) edges collapse to run size — degenerate and non-transferable. Saturation ratio ρ = |E_D| / (n choose 2) separates regimes: observed ~0.01 (informative), inferred ~1.0 (useless). For amg: `depends_on` edges should default to declared grade (user/agent states the dependency), not inferred.
166. **Provenance collapse is the fourth failure mode — and amg already prevents it (#041)** — MemClaw identifies provenance collapse (memories can't trace to origin) as a fleet-memory failure mode. amg's bi-temporal edge tracking already records validAt/recordedAt/source. Adding `derivedFrom` chains via depends_on edges completes the provenance graph: every fact traces back to its original source. Zero-cost extension of existing edge infrastructure.

167. **Bottleneck score = fan_out / max(fan_in, 1) reveals single points of failure in derived knowledge (c339)** — A node with high fan-out relative to fan-in means many downstream facts depend on it but it has few sources. If this node is wrong, the error cascades broadly but is hard to detect because few upstream sources can contradict it. derivation_lineage_report flags bottleneck_score > 1 with ⚠ in summary. This is a novel metric not found in any graph memory library — it identifies epistemic fragility in knowledge graphs using only local edge topology.

168. **Entropy scan shape descriptors capture multi-scale topology personality (c336-342)** — A single Rényi α value is a snapshot. A SWEEP across α ∈ {0.5, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, ∞} produces a curve whose shape (monotonicity, range, convergence CV) reveals the graph's structural personality: flat=regular, steep=hierarchical, convex with knee=community+hub mix. entropy_depth_profile adds BFS-layer entropy to distinguish local clustering from global topology. Together with entropy_explain (5-layer human interpretation), these 4 APIs make entropy analysis accessible without a PhD in spectral graph theory.
169. **Contradiction detection pipeline ordering matters — dedup starves contradiction (#041)** — MemClaw discovered that a synchronous near-duplicate detector rejects contradictory writes before the async contradiction detector runs. Since "X is A" vs "X is B" is near-identical text, the dedup gate fires first. Fix: contradiction detection must run before dedup, or the dedup threshold must widen for writes carrying structural metadata. Pipeline ordering, not algorithm improvement.

---

170. **Three orthogonal meanings of "multi-scale" in graph entropy (#042)** — (1) Parameter multi-scale: varying Rényi α / Tsallis q changes what entropy detects (hubs vs communities vs regularity). (2) Graph reduction multi-scale (arXiv:2510.11524): spectral coarse-graining changes resolution at which structure is visible — multi-scale entropy predicts link formation R²>0.9 vs ~0.5 single-scale. (3) Depth multi-scale (SREGK): h-layer expansion subgraphs change locality from ego-network to global. For amg: parameter sweep first (highest ROI), graph reduction + depth as future extensions.
171. **Curve shape descriptors ARE the fingerprint — not raw entropy values (#042)** — The trinity of monotonicity (is the graph regular?), convexity (structural transition?), knee position (community dominance scale?) compresses a 10-20 point curve into 3 interpretable features. Combined with curve area (structural content) and max-min gap (heterogeneity range), these 5 numbers form a more robust fingerprint than raw values. Verified: Star gap=2.27, Complete gap=0.0, Path gap=0.15 — clear separation.
172. **Tsallis q detects community modularity that Rényi α misses (#042)** — Tsallis entropy at high q captures whether communities are balanced (additive, q→1 limit) or dominated by one large community (non-extensive, q>1). This is a signal Rényi alone cannot isolate. For graphs with strong community structure, the 2D parameter space (α × q) creates a surface where ridges along axes reveal hub-dominance vs community-modularity.
173. **Multi-scale compression entropy predicts link formation (arXiv:2510.11524, #042)** — Entropy at 40-60% graph reduction is significantly better predictor of link formation than full-graph entropy (R²>0.9 vs ~0.5). Graphs with low entropy at coarse resolution have predictable growth patterns. For amg users: entropy_scan doubles as link predictability estimator — high-compressibility graphs grow predictably, low-compressibility graphs need more monitoring.
174. **Deep Rényi h-layer expansion connects entropy_scan to entropy_contribution (#042)** — SREGK's h-layer expansion gives each node a depth-profile of Rényi entropies. Nodes with flat depth-profiles are in homogeneous neighborhoods (replaceable). Nodes with steeply changing profiles are at structural boundaries (important connectors). Combined with entropy_contribution() (c306): high contribution + steep depth-profile = critical structural node. This closes the local-to-global entropy gradient loop.

---

175. **Memory is becoming a protocol primitive — not just a tool endpoint (#043)** — memorywire's thesis: wrapping remember/recall/forget as three opaque MCP tools is lossy. The type taxonomy collapses to a string, governance sits outside the protocol, and lifecycle is invisible. But MCP-tool wrapper is still the pragmatic adoption path (~10 lines glue). Strategic play: ship MCP tool wrapper now with memorywire-compatible field names (`agent_id`, `type`, `confidence`, `source`, `approval_required`), upgrade to formal MCP extension when memorywire v0.5 stabilizes. Forward compatibility is free if field names align today.
176. **The MCP 2026-07-28 stateless core eliminates every objection to MCP memory servers (#043)** — No sessions, no sticky routing, no shared session store. Every request carries client info in `_meta`. Deploy behind any load balancer. Route by `Mcp-Method` header. Tasks primitive enables async consolidation as first-class MCP workflow. For amg: the MCP server is a stateless wrapper around an already-stateless library. Zero-friction deployment.
177. **amg is invisible in the fastest-growing distribution channel (#043)** — 10+ OpenClaw memory plugins exist (Cognee, PlugMem, graph-memory, Neo4j, Maximem, MemOS Cloud, Lossless Claw, memU, Memory LanceDB, Mem Arch). amg has ZERO presence despite 925+ APIs and 6622 tests. Every day without a plugin, users choose a competitor with fewer capabilities. The plugin skeleton is ~200 lines (SessionStart→inject, PostToolUse→capture, SessionEnd→consolidate + slash commands). `/entropy` command is a unique differentiator no competitor offers.
178. **memorywire's RRF fusion independently validates amg's architecture (#043)** — memorywire uses RRF (k=60) as default fusion across multiple memory backends. Proven: recall@5=1.000 under 1-of-NN adversarial attack where MAX collapses to 0.500. amg already uses RRF in `rrf_classification()` (c326) and `hybrid_classification()`. The memorywire paper provides independent academic validation. For multi-backend amg deployments: RRF is the proven default.
179. **The governance gap is amg's unpicked lock (#043)** — memorywire's Co-memorize diff-and-approve pattern (writes staged behind sentinel, human reviews before commit) is a feature no OpenClaw plugin has. amg's `write_governance_check()` already exists but is not user-facing. Adding `approval_required` parameter to MCP `remember` tool + `/pending` slash command = governance capability that Mem0, Cognee, PlugMem, and graph-memory all lack. Implementable today without waiting for memorywire v0.5.

---

180. **Claude Code's memory is deliberately primitive — Markdown + keyword search by design (#044)** — Leaked source (512K lines TS, v2.1.88) reveals 4-layer architecture: CLAUDE.md (manual static rules) → Auto Memory (4 categories, 200-line index cap) → Auto Dream (background consolidation resolving temporal refs + contradictions + stale entries) → KAIROS (unshipped always-on daemon, 150+ source references). NO semantic search. NO embeddings. The 200-line cap forces aggressive compression. Auto Dream triggers after >24h + >5 sessions. This is a deliberate simplicity trade-off: human-readable, git-committable, predictable. For amg positioning: "Claude Code's memory works for 200 lines. amg works for 200,000." The sophistication gap is amg's commercial opening.
181. **Code KGs and agent memory graphs solve different halves of the same problem — nobody unifies them (#044)** — Code structure tools (Codebase-Memory, CodeGraph, RepoGraph, Prometheus) answer "what does the code look like?" Agent experience tools (agentmemory 23.8K★, memsearch, Conare) answer "what has the agent done?" Nobody answers "how does what the agent did relate to what the code looks like?" The two communities don't overlap: Code KG builders come from SE/PL backgrounds (tree-sitter, LSP, AST); agent memory builders come from ML/NLP backgrounds (embeddings, RAG, graphs). amg already has the infrastructure: graph + bi-temporal + provenance + entropy + 925 APIs. Adding code node types (~40 lines type defs) + `explainCode(nodeId)` (~30 lines wrapping trace_derivation) = zero new algorithm, just new edge types. Total ~70 lines to open a new market category.
182. **Token economics will force the convergence of code structure and agent experience (#044)** — CodeGraph: 89% fewer tool calls, 60% cheaper, 69% fewer tokens. Codebase-Memory: 121× average token reduction across 372 questions. agentmemory: 23.8K stars shows persistent memory demand. Combined (projected): ~97% token reduction + zero context-rebuild time. Prometheus's working memory (retains explored contexts across reasoning steps) is the navigation-temporal-continuity primitive amg lacks. Adding `kind="explored"` edges with temporal metadata = "I was here 3h ago, found X, Y, Z. Has anything changed?" — that's `whatChanged(sinceTimestamp)` in the prototype.
183. **Procedural memory for code is the most commercially valuable gap — and code is the ideal training ground (#044)** — Mem0 2026 report: procedural memory for coding agents is "early-stage." Nobody learns workflows from observed success (team runs `npm test` before commit, PRs follow conventional commits, tests go in `__tests__/`). AEL paper shows skill extraction degrades -15% in noisy domains. Code is LOW-noise (tests pass/fail, builds succeed/fail) — ideal for pattern extraction. For amg: `compress_to_skill()` with code-specific mode (observe → validate via test pass rate → promote to procedural_pattern node). Code domain's binary success signal makes it the highest-ROI application of skill extraction.
184. **The unified code-aware memory graph needs only 3 new APIs on top of amg's existing infrastructure (#044)** — (1) `explainCode(nodeId)` — traces code node to decisions/bugfixes/dependents, ~30 lines wrapping trace_derivation. (2) `impactAnalysis(nodeId)` — BFS cascade through calls/imports/depends_on edges, ~25 lines wrapping existing traversal. (3) `recordCodeDecision(codeIds, content, confidence)` — creates experience node + links to code nodes via decided_by edges, ~20 lines. Total: ~75 lines of new code. Zero new dependencies. Zero algorithmic changes. All existing entropy/provenance/classification APIs work on code nodes by default. This is the lowest-effort, highest-impact extension in amg's roadmap.

185. **Silent prediction extraction bugs make "honest results" meaningless (c340)** — classification_benchmark reported ~17% accuracy for most methods and 0% for graph/compare. The notes said "honest results: most methods score ~20-33%." Root cause: prediction extraction used `best_ref_index` which doesn't exist in ANY method's return dict — silently fell through to default 0 (=star reference). Each method returns its winner under a different key (`best_match`/`best_ref`/`consensus_best`). Combined with an unsupported `include_quarantined` kwarg causing TypeError in graph_classification → all predictions were None. After fix: all 8 methods achieve ~100% on canonical topologies. Lesson: **test the test infrastructure itself** — a benchmark that returns numbers without crashing can still be completely wrong.
186. **Noise robustness reveals method complementarity invisible at zero noise (c341)** — At zero noise, all 8 classification methods achieve ~100% accuracy — they look interchangeable. Under 10% edge perturbation, RRF is most robust (AUC=0.67) while degree-based methods degrade fastest. Tree topology is most fragile (AUC=0.25) — single edge change disconnects the graph. Cycle is most resilient (AUC=0.69) — structural redundancy. Breakpoint detection (first noise level <0.8 accuracy) shows all methods fail between 10-20% noise. This data informs method selection: use RRF for noisy/real-world graphs, use any method for clean canonical structures. Per-query deterministic seeds (derived from base_seed + noise_level + topology_index + query_index) ensure reproducibility despite classification methods consuming global RNG state.
187. **Memory observability is the biggest gap in OTel GenAI SemConv — and AMG's unique positioning opportunity (#034)** — The OTel GenAI spec has 22 libraries covering inference and 13 covering tool execution, but only 2 with reference coverage for memory operations. The OpenLLMetry RFC proposes 5 memory span types (store/retrieve/search/update/delete) with attributes like `gen_ai.memory.type`, `gen_ai.memory.store`, `gen_ai.memory.items_stored`, `gen_ai.memory.relevance_score`, `gen_ai.memory.hit`. Prototype validated: full CRUD agent turn emits proper span tree (invoke_agent → chat + memory.store + memory.search + chat). No competing graph-memory system has native OTel observability. AMG + OTel instrumentation = first-mover advantage in observable agent memory.
188. **OTel GenAI SemConv moves fast — pin, map, dual-emit is the survival strategy (#034)** — v1.37→v1.41 each changed conventions: system→provider.name, per-message events→aggregated attributes, invoke_agent split into CLIENT/INTERNAL, reasoning tokens added, execute_tool naming changed. Spec is still `Status: Development` with no stabilization timeline. Four practices: (1) Pin instrumentation version + convention commit, (2) Thin mapping layer between raw keys and dashboards, (3) Never invent custom `gen_ai.*` keys, (4) Dual-emit across renames for one retention window. The 5 sampling-critical attributes that MUST be set at span creation: `operation.name`, `provider.name`, `request.model`, `server.address`, `server.port`.

---

191. **Temporal hierarchy is the missing abstraction layer — every SOTA system has it, amg doesn't (#045)** — TiMem (5 levels), HiMem (Episode+Note), EverMemOS (MemCells→MemScenes→Profile), H-Mem (daily→weekly→monthly). All converge on multi-level temporal abstraction with "descend-when-necessary" retrieval. amg has bi-temporal edges and entropy-based forgetting but no abstraction hierarchy — the graph is temporally flat. SummaryTree layer (~150 lines) on top of existing graph closes this gap. Progressive consolidation |M_i|≤|M_{i-1}| means higher levels have fewer nodes. Best-effort retrieval (Note first → descend to Episode) saves ~21% tokens vs hybrid with minimal accuracy loss.
192. **Compression residuals recover precision that summaries destroy — for zero extra LLM calls (#045)** — ProGraph's key insight: narrative summaries paraphrase away dates, quantities, named items. But co-extracting atomic facts alongside the summary in the same prompt recovers them at zero API cost. On LoCoMo: disabling residuals costs -8.6pp. Query "what date did Caroline take pottery class?" fails on profile ("passionate potter since 2023") but succeeds on residual ("May 7, 2023"). For amg: every consolidate() call should output (summary, residuals[]) tuples. ~20 lines to add to consolidation pipeline.
193. **Memory reconsolidation closes the write-read feedback loop — retrieval failure becomes a learning signal (#045)** — HiMem's Memory Reconsolidation: when Note Memory fails but Episode Memory provides evidence, extract new knowledge and write it back. Classification: independent→ADD, extendable→UPDATE, contradictory→DELETE/REPLACE. This is auto_heal_gaps() extended to temporal hierarchy. Gain compounds: system gets better with use, not worse. +5.85% on Note Memory alone. For amg: ~40 lines to extend existing gap detection to promote evidence up the hierarchy.
194. **Query complexity awareness multiplies retrieval efficiency — and amg already has the router (#045)** — TiMem's recall planner selects hierarchy levels based on query complexity. Simple → profile level. Complex → segment level. amg already has entropy_guided_query_route() (Cycle 287) that routes based on graph entropy. Wiring: high entropy → start at segment level, low entropy → start at profile/week. Zero new algorithm — just connect entropy router output to SummaryTree starting level. This is the kind of emergent capability amg's integrated design enables: entropy framework + temporal hierarchy = adaptive retrieval depth.
195. **Foresight signals in MemCells are unique to EverMemOS — and are a form of prospective memory (#045)** — EverMemOS stores time-bounded predictions ("user might ask about X next") alongside episodic traces. No other system does this explicitly. This maps to amg's planned `kind="repair_pattern"` node type (Research #018) — both are forms of prospective memory. Storing anticipated future queries as graph nodes enables pre-emptive retrieval preparation. ~30 lines to add `foresight` field to MemoryNode metadata.

189. **Grid-search weight optimization reveals modality informativeness hierarchy (c348)** — Equal weights (1:1:1) are suboptimal when modalities have different discriminative power. For path-vs-cycle classification, fingerprint alone achieves 70% while degree and spectral each achieve only 50%. Grid search over weight_resolution=5 produces 215 combinations and correctly identifies the optimal tuple. Key design: cached per-modality scoring precomputes degree/spectral/fingerprint scores once per query×reference pair, then evaluates all weight combos in O(combos × queries) without re-running classification. Tie-breaking by weight Shannon entropy prefers balanced weights when accuracy is equal. Completes the build → evaluate → **learn** → deploy loop.
190. **Confusion matrix with per-class metrics is the diagnostic instrument classification was missing (c349)** — Macro accuracy hides failure patterns. A full N×N confusion matrix with per-class precision/recall/F1 reveals WHICH classes are confused and HOW OFTEN. `most_confused_with` per class and `most_confused_pairs` globally provide actionable debugging targets. `hardest_queries` (queries where fewest methods succeed) identify outlier graphs that need investigation. The report works with all 8 classification methods and accepts user-provided (query, expected_label) pairs — unlike benchmark which auto-generates canonical topologies. This is the standard ML evaluation practice (scikit-learn's classification_report equivalent) that no graph library had.

196. **LOOCV for reference graphs tests "can we identify an UNSEEN topology?" — not just "how accurate are we?" (#046)** — Standard k-fold CV randomly partitions data. LOOCV for reference graphs has unique semantics: hold out one reference R_i, learn weights from remaining N-1, classify R_i. This directly tests whether the entropy fingerprint captures structural essence vs. memorizing specific reference instances. With 6 canonical topologies, it's 6 iterations (trivially cheap). The per-fold diagnostic ("held out 'path' → classified as 'star', gap=0.012") pinpoints exactly which structural features are insufficient. For amg: `classification_loocv()` = 17th classification API, ~30 lines.
197. **Graph distance scores are systematically under-confident — temperature T<1 needed (#046)** — ECE measurement reveals raw graph similarity scores (JSD, spectral, fingerprint L2) cluster in narrow bands, producing 52% ECE. Optimal temperature T=0.5 (sharpening). This mirrors the GNN calibration literature: GNNs are under-confident (Wang et al., 2021), unlike image classifiers which are over-confident. Root cause: graph distance metrics compress output space. Without normalization+temperature, raw scores can't be interpreted as probabilities. `classification_calibrate()` = 18th classification API: grid-search T to minimize ECE, return reliability diagram data. Makes all classification APIs' confidence values trustworthy for rejection thresholds.
198. **.632 bootstrap degenerates when apparent error is zero — which it always is for graph fingerprint matching (#046)** — The .632 correction assumes LOO error > apparent error. But graph entropy fingerprinting achieves 0% apparent error (exact match always identifies correct reference). So .632 = 0.368×0 + 0.632×loo_error = fixed 63.2% of LOO error. The adaptive property is lost — it becomes a constant shrinkage factor. Still useful (more conservative), but not bias-correcting in the intended sense. For honest reporting: use percentile bootstrap for n≥30, .632 as upper bound for n<30, and always report sample size alongside CI width.
199. **McNemar's test shows 60 queries is insufficient for p<0.05 significance between similar methods (#046)** — Degree vs fingerprint with n01=11, n10=7 gives χ²=0.5 — far below the 3.841 threshold for p<0.05. This means reporting "fingerprint outperforms degree" is statistically unsupported at typical sample sizes. Two implications: (1) classification_compare() should include significance tests, not just accuracy comparisons; (2) the benchmark should scale to 200+ queries per topology to achieve statistical power. For amg npm launch: reporting McNemar alongside accuracy is a credibility signal that no competitor provides.
200. **Prototype selection (ENN/CCCD) from 50-year NN literature maps directly to graph reference optimization (#046)** — Edited Nearest Neighbor removes references misclassified by their k nearest neighbors — directly answering "do we need both path AND cycle?" Class Cover Catch Digraph finds the minimum set of covering references per family. Both are O(N²) similarity computations — trivial for amg's reference sets. `optimize_reference_set()` = 19th classification API. Especially valuable when users accumulate 50-100 reference graphs and need to identify redundancy. No graph library in npm/PyPI has reference set optimization.

201. **The recompute tax is the hidden cost of entropy-aware agent memory (#047)** — Every agent write changes graph topology. Without incremental entropy, each classification/retrieval/health check requires O(n³) full eigendecomposition. FINGER reduces this to O(Δn+Δm) — constant per edge update. At 1000 nodes, this is the difference between ~300ms blocking and <1ms streaming. Implication: entropy-aware features (24-API classification suite, entropy-weighted retrieval, TemporalEntropyTracker) can operate in real-time on streaming agent memory. No competitor (Mem0, Zep, Letta) has graph entropy at all, let alone streaming entropy. amg's entire 40+ entropy API surface becomes real-time-capable with ~200 lines of FINGER integration.
202. **Quadratic proxy Q is the universal O(1) graph health signal (#047)** — Q = 1 - tr(L²)/tr²(L) captures structural complexity in one number. High Q = diverse, well-distributed. Low Q = dominated by few nodes. Because Q updates in O(Δ) via Theorem 2, it serves as an O(1) real-time health monitor: every edge add/delete updates Q in constant time. The Q trajectory over time is a free graph health time series. Anomaly detection = "Q dropped >3σ from rolling mean." This extends entropy_explain's health layer from static snapshot to streaming monitor at zero additional computation cost.
203. **FINGER enables streaming anomaly detection — not just batch snapshots (#047)** — JS distance between consecutive graph states detects anomalous writes (contradiction bursts, injection attacks, topic shifts). Currently amg computes JS distance offline from snapshots. With FINGER incremental, each incoming batch produces a JS score in O(Δ). Write governance can reject batches with JS > θ before polluting the graph. Paper validated on Wikipedia networks (1.8M nodes) and DoS detection. Agent memory is smaller but higher-stakes — a rejected bad write is worth more than a detected anomaly.
204. **SLaQ and FINGER are complementary — different regimes (#047)** — SLaQ (stochastic Lanczos quadrature) for very large one-shot snapshots (n>10⁵). FINGER for streaming sequences of incremental updates. Radial Projection for no-eigenvalue linear computation. Strategy for amg: exact eigendecomposition for small reference graphs (<200 nodes, classification suite), FINGER for streaming agent write path, SLaQ reserved for web-scale one-shot comparisons. Amg already has exact path (von_neumann_entropy); FINGER adds streaming path.
205. **"Are We Ready" validates localized maintenance over global reorganization (#047)** — Comprehensive 2026 study (12 systems × 11 datasets, arXiv:2606.24775) finds O7: "Localized maintenance is more cost-efficient than global reorganization." Graph-based methods (Zep, Cognee) handle updates best but have highest latency (155s for Zep). FINGER addresses this exact bottleneck: O(Δ) vs O(n+m) brings graph-based memory into the latency regime of flat vector stores. Conservative consolidation beats aggressive merging (O11) — another argument for incremental FINGER updates over periodic full recomputation.

206. **Graph traversal IS multi-hop reasoning — and AMG already has the substrate (#048)** — Every major 2025-2026 system (HippoRAG2, PathRAG, GFM-RAG, StepChain, A-MEM, MemGraphRAG) converges: multi-hop reasoning = graph traversal with intelligent pruning. AMG already has typed edges + bi-temporal + entropy-weighted traversal + provenance chains + conditioned_traverse + trace_derivation. The missing piece: a user-facing `multi_hop_reason(question, seeds, max_depth)` API orchestrating existing primitives. ~80 lines wrapping conditioned_traverse + entropy_weighted_retrieval + evidence path collection. First npm library with entropy-guided multi-hop reasoning.
207. **Personalized PageRank replaces BFS for production retrieval — 15 lines of numpy (#048)** — HippoRAG2's PPR simulates activation spreading from seed nodes. High-weight paths get more activation, noisy paths die out. 10-30× cheaper, 6-13× faster than iterative retrieval. Implementation: power iteration ~15 lines. For amg: `personalized_pagerank(seeds, weights, damping=0.85)` complements conditioned_traverse. PPR for broad recall, conditioned_traverse for precise filtered paths. Together they span the retrieval strategy space.
208. **GFM-RAG's pre-trained GNN is a fundamental shift — AMG's response is zero-training positioning (#048)** — GFM-RAG (NeurIPS 2025, ICLR 2026) trains 8M→34M param GNN on 60 KGs (14M triples) for zero-shot multi-hop reasoning. Strategic response: AMG's "no training data, zero dependencies, human-interpretable entropy metrics" is a different market. GFM-RAG's GNN is a black box; AMG's entropy_explain() is glass box. For npm: "no training data needed" beats "download our 34M model" for developer adoption.
209. **A-MEM's memory evolution is what AMG's adaptive forgetting COULD become (#048)** — A-MEM (NeurIPS 2025, 930★): when new memories arrive, OLD memories get retroactively enriched with new semantic context. Not just forgetting — enrichment. AMG has entity_resolution + merge_nodes (merge duplicates) but lacks enrich_node (retroactive metadata update from inference). ~40 lines. Makes AMG's memory genuinely agentic (self-organizing) vs managed (externally controlled).
210. **The GraphRAG cost cliff collapsed — graph retrieval is now production-viable (#048)** — 2024: $33,000 to index 5GB for Microsoft GraphRAG. Mid-2025: LazyGraphRAG = 0.1% (~$33). The "graphs are too expensive" objection is dead. Real cost shifted from indexing to query latency. Validates AMG's focus on incremental/streaming entropy + pre-computed fingerprints. Latency is the new moat, not indexing cost.
211. **Retrieval ≠ Memory: the 50-point MemoryArena gap is the most important number in agent memory evaluation (#051)** — Models scoring 90%+ on LoCoMo (passive recall) plummet to 40-60% on MemoryArena (interdependent multi-session tasks). LoCoMo tests "Was fact X mentioned?" while MemoryArena tests "Use fact X from session 1 to make decision in session 3." For amg-bench: every question must test active utility, not just retrieval. The 50-point gap means published LoCoMo scores are necessary but wildly insufficient. amg's multi_hop_reason + spreading_activation APIs are exactly what MemoryArena-style tasks stress.
212. **FAMA reveals that all systems have a stale memory problem — 15-43 point penalties (#051)** — Forgetting-Aware Memory Accuracy (FAMA = max(0, MPA - λ·(1-FAA))) penalizes reliance on outdated info. Across all tested systems, applying FAMA reduces scores by 15-43 points. Systems that look identical on standard accuracy reshuffle dramatically under FAMA. Key finding: memory agents' forgetting penalties INCREASE with longer timelines (18.2 weekly → 29.5 quarterly) — the more memory scales, the worse forgetting gets. amg's adaptive_forgetting + entropy-weighted retrieval directly addresses this. For amg-bench: paired presence + forgetting criteria per question.
213. **Agent capability > tool sophistication: filesystem beats specialized memory tools (#051)** — Letta showed a simple grep+search_files agent scores 74% on LoCoMo, beating Mem0's graph variant (68.5%). Implication: evaluation is system-level, not component-level. LLM post-training determines tool-use effectiveness more than tool design. For amg-bench: provide a reference agent loop, let systems plug in memory backends (like STATE-Bench's "bring your own memory"). Don't evaluate retrieval backends in isolation.
214. **No community-standard harness exists — this is amg-bench's GLUE moment (#051)** — arXiv:2603.07670 survey: "The field still lacks a community-standard evaluation harness. Each benchmark uses its own datasets, metrics, and protocols." The survey authors propose a GLUE-style shared leaderboard. The benchmark trio (LoCoMo + LongMemEval + BEAM) is the de facto standard, but nobody provides a unified harness. amg-bench can fill this gap — especially for graph-memory + entropy + classification niches that no existing benchmark covers.
215. **STATE-Bench's enterprise procedural evaluation is the complement to LoCoMo's conversational recall (#051)** — Microsoft STATE-Bench (450 tasks, 3 domains) tests whether memory improves agents on realistic enterprise tasks: procedural compliance, state mutation, user experience. Key finding: even GPT-5.1 without memory completes <50% reliably; pass^5 for travel is ~30%. This is the production-readiness benchmark. amg-bench should support both conversational (LoCoMo-style) and procedural (STATE-Bench-style) tracks.

216. **Memory poisoning is temporally decoupled — Feb injection, April damage, attacker long gone (#052)** — OWASP ASI06 formalizes memory poisoning as distinct from prompt injection. Persistence in substrate (memory store) not surface (prompt). Defense requires lifecycle governance across 5 layers. amg = only system with infrastructure for all 5: write_governance_check (L1) + provenance/lineage suite (L2/L5) + entropy_weighted_retrieval (L3) + StreamingGraph anomaly detection (L4).
217. **Provenance laundering is money laundering for memory — compression pipelines are the washing machine (#052)** — Agent compression reduces toxicity 0.6→0.0852 (below 0.5 detection) while harmful semantics survive. Proposed detect_provenance_laundering() combines source trust + compression method + semantic matching. amg's bi-temporal + trace_derivation records the transformation chain.
218. **Channel C4 (skill synthesis) is highest-impact attack vector — poisoned skills execute code (#052)** — MPBench V-S4: no validation for skill creation. compress_to_skill() MUST require approval_required=true. Design constraint, not tuning parameter.
219. **MemSecBench 84.2% persistence / 56.1% selective repair — the gap is amg's opportunity (#052)** — propagate_correction + trace_derivation_impact = selective memory surgery. No competitor has dependency-edge-aware correction.
220. **"More memory = more exploitable" validates governance-first — A-MemGuard dual-memory maps to FastAppendQueue (#052)** — 95% ASR reduction via shadow→active dual-memory. System-1/System-2 split now has equal security and performance justification.

221. **The "just use long context" objection has a precise expiration date — 10 turns (#057)** — Cost crossover analysis (arXiv:2603.04814) shows memory systems become cheaper at ~10 interaction turns (100K context). At 20 turns: 26% cheaper. At 10K sessions/day: 93% cheaper ($161K/month savings on GPT-5-mini). Long context is cheaper only for <10 turn sessions with small context. The math is unambiguous. For amg README: "If your agent serves >10 turns, you're overpaying for context."
222. **Retrieval ≠ Utilization — the 50-point MemoryArena gap is the real moat (#057)** — LoCoMo measures "did the system find the fact?" MemoryArena measures "did the system USE the fact for a decision?" Models scoring 90%+ on LoCoMo drop to 40-60% on MemoryArena. The 50-point gap means published LoCoMo scores are necessary but wildly insufficient. amg's multi_hop_reason() + spreading_activation() are designed for utilization, not just retrieval. This is the positioning differentiator vs flat vector stores.
223. **Context windows will plateau — the industry is shifting to memory architecture (#057)** — Zylos Research (2026): "The future favors intelligence over size." Claude Sonnet 5 (1M, June 2026) and Gemini 3 Pro (1M) are the practical ceiling. Beyond 10M: geometric cost escalation with diminishing returns. The shift is already happening: inference-time scaling + context management + hybrid memory-augmented systems. amg is on the right side of this shift.
224. **δ-mem's 8×8 matrix proves memory can be parametric, not just retrieval-based (#057)** — δ-mem (arXiv:2605.12357) stores conversation history in 64 numbers that modify attention. Frozen backbone. Context wiped clean → model still retains information. +31% MemoryAgentBench, +20% LoCoMo. This is a third paradigm alongside (1) long context and (2) external memory (amg). They complement: δ-mem captures implicit patterns; amg stores explicit facts with provenance + entropy + temporal tracking. Different abstraction levels.
225. **"More context = worse performance" is the most counterintuitive finding for product teams (#057)** — The U-shaped attention curve means adding MORE context can DECREASE accuracy for information already in context. At 50 documents in context, 42/50 are in the dead zone (<60% accuracy). At 100 documents, 70% of context is effectively wasted. For amg: entropy-weighted retrieval isn't just about saving tokens — it's about quality. Retrieving the right 5K tokens beats stuffing 200K tokens every time.

226. **The "diagnose → explain → act" trilogy pattern is now established across two subsystems (c406b+407)** — retrieval_quality_audit() → retrieval_quality_explain() completes the diagnostic-drilldown pair for retrieval. attention_distribution() → attention_rebalance_plan() completes the diagnostic-action pair for attention. Both follow the same meta-pattern: set-level diagnosis, per-node drill-down, per-node action plan with estimated impact. This mirrors the standard ML evaluation pattern (classification_report → confusion_matrix → hyperparameter tuning) applied to memory graph operations. The trilogy makes the system self-improving: audit finds problems, explain identifies causes, plan proposes fixes. Per-action Gini delta estimation (Cycle 407) is particularly novel — no competitor estimates the impact of individual memory interventions.

227. **Bi-temporal queries need 3 modes: knowledge, truth, certain (c412)** — What the agent believed at time T (knowledge) differs from what was objectively true (truth). The intersection (certain) is what the agent knew AND was correct. The classic Alice scenario: agent records Alice@Acme at T0, Alice moves at T1, agent discovers at T2. `bitemporal_as_of(T1+ε, mode="knowledge")` returns Acme (agent didn't know yet). `mode="truth"` returns Google. `mode="certain"` returns nothing (agent wrong). This trichotomy is absent from Mem0, Zep, Graphiti — they all conflate knowledge with truth. Named `bitemporal_as_of` to coexist with `query_as_of` (graph snapshot API).
228. **Non-destructive forgetting forecast is the planning tool that forgetting_curve() can't be (c413)** — `forgetting_curve()` UPDATES weights (destructive). `forgetting_forecast()` projects forward WITHOUT modifying — solving analytically for time-to-threshold. 4 risk zones (critical <24h, high <72h, medium <168h, low >168h) enable proactive intervention. Population summary (at-risk count, median TTT, earliest crossing) drives batch maintenance scheduling. This is the preventive counterpart to reactive forgetting — no competitor has predictive forgetting analysis.
229. **Greedy Marginal Contribution turns quality diagnostics into actionable correction (c414)** — `retrieval_quality_rerank()` picks nodes greedily by marginal value across 4 dimensions (coverage gain, diversity gain, freshness, redundancy penalty). O(n²) but n is small (retrieval sets of 10-50). Audit before/after with improvement deltas enables A/B comparison. Weight overrides enable domain tuning (coverage-heavy for KGs, diversity-heavy for recommendations). This completes the retrieval quality lifecycle: audit → explain → **rerank** (correct).
230. **Multi-set comparison completes the retrieval quality family — A/B testing for retrieval strategies (c415)** — `retrieval_quality_compare()` runs audit() on 2+ result sets and produces pairwise Jaccard overlap matrix, unique/common node analysis, per-dimension winners, agreement classification (5 levels). Low overlap → ensemble fusion advice. High overlap → single strategy suffices. This is the retrieval equivalent of `classification_compare()`. The complete family: audit → explain → rerank → compare. No competitor has ANY retrieval quality API, let alone a 4-member family spanning diagnose → drill-down → correct → compare.
227. **MCP 2026-07-28 stateless core makes session affinity obsolete — every request is independent (#059)** — The initialize/initialized handshake and Mcp-Session-Id header are gone. Protocol version, client info, and capabilities travel inline in `_meta`. This means MCP servers deploy behind any HTTP load balancer — no sticky sessions, no shared session store. For amg-mcp: the server is already stateless at the library level; the SDK v2 upgrade removes the session wrapper. Horizontal scaling is now trivial. The spec also formalized Tasks primitive for async operations (consolidation as first-class MCP workflow).
228. **The distribution gap IS the opportunity — amg's OWASP suite has zero competitors in MCP ecosystem (#059)** — AgentAudit found 118 security findings across 68 MCP packages. Zero memory MCP servers have built-in trust scoring, quarantine, or provenance laundering detection. The `mcp` keyword on npm auto-discovers into registry crawlers. Multi-channel distribution (npm + PyPI + registry PR + mcp-submit for 10+ directories) from day one maximizes visibility. The fragmented discovery ecosystem means being everywhere matters more than being perfect in one place.
229. **Dual distribution — npm MCP server + OpenClaw plugin — captures both developer and non-developer audiences (#059)** — npm users want clean API and flexible config. OpenClaw users want `openclaw plugins install` and it just works. The ~200-line OpenClaw plugin wraps the MCP server with auto-hooks (before_prompt_build for memory injection, after_tool_call for capture). No code duplication — plugin proxies to MCP server. This is the highest-ROI next step because it opens a distribution channel the MCP server alone can't reach. 10+ memory plugins already exist in OpenClaw; amg is absent despite 875+ APIs.
230. **amg-mcp's architecture is already migration-ready — SDK v2 codemod + keyword update = publishable in 2-3 days (#059)** — Current codebase has dual-transport (stdio + HTTP), 16 registered tools, 1718 lines TS, 122 tests. Migration path: `npx @modelcontextprotocol/codemod@beta v1-to-v2 .` handles API renames, remove session management code from http-server.ts, add `mcp` keyword to package.json, write registry-ready README. The codemod is official and handles standard renames like `.tool()` → `registerTool()`.

231. **Abstention 双门的 evidence≥3 门限不是调参——是语义分界 (C448)** — 二路弱平局 [1,1] 不该 abstain：amg 有原则性消歧器（bitemporal -seq latest-wins = knowledge-update 语义）；强平局 [2,2] 恰是 update 签名；只有 ≥3 路弱平 [1,1,1] 才是散落证据=均匀猜测域=LongMemEval `_abs` 惩罚的幻觉区。门是纯后检索决策 → 阈值 sweep 零额外检索成本（每 question 一次 retrieval 扫全部阈值）。
232. **子串相关性污染排序——词边界+屈折形态是检索底线，token 效率是第二记分牌 (C447)** — "love" 子串命中 "lovely" 导致 answer 错误消息：相关性匹配必须词边界 + 屈折后缀（s/es/ed/ing + e-deletion + 辅音加倍），拒绝派生形态（love⊄lovely）。LongMemEval 的 tokens/query 让 graph-precision 检索可量化对标：Mem0 ~6787 tok/query vs amg 目标 <2000——这是除准确率外第二张竞品对比表。
233. **幻影指标比功能漂移更隐蔽——台账数字必须 count-from-truth (#068)** — C446 抓到功能漂移（声称 ✅ 但真身仓缺失），#068 抓到指标漂移（"TS 7349" 语言根本不存在）。共性根因：手写台账从不回链 git 可验证事实。修法制度化：knowledge-org 02:00 轮改用脚本从真身仓实时计数（publish_namespace_audit.py 雏形），手写数字仅允许引用脚本输出。对外声称（README/博客/registry）一旦引用幻影数字，被社区审计 = 信誉事故。
234. **npm 裸名是稀缺资产，scoped 包是唯一结构性免疫 (#068)** — LightHaru 2026-05-22 抢注 `agent-memory-graph`（TypeScript graph memory，10 天 19 版后弃坑，1 star）——名字拿不回。抢注成本极低、先到先得，任何准备 npm 发布的项目都该在 Day 1 就占位（哪怕空 publish 或 scoped）。@robertsong2019/* scoped 名随 GitHub org 语义对齐 PyPI Trusted Publisher 流程。教训：发布计划的名宇可用性检查必须在 README 终稿前，不是发布前夜。
235. **“TS port” 伪任务的识别价值——选任务前先验真身资产 (#068)** — HEARTBEAT “TS port of Python APIs” 建立在幻影指标上：真 TS 化 = 55k 行/831 方法从零重写，不是 port。与 08-15 教训（选任务前先 grep 真身代码验证缺失）同源：任务清单条目本身可能是漂移产物。规则升级：涉及“已有资产”假设的任务，启动前先跑事实核查（文件存在性/计数可复现）。
236. **Adversarial 题型的词汇重叠悖论 (C452)** — LoCoMo cat5 adversarial 问题的 keyword-evidence 分数反而是全题型最高（best_score 中位 3 vs 事实题 2，94% ≥2）——它们是"真实体+假事件"构型，实体词全部命中，只有事件本身是编造的。任何基于证据强度的置信门（熵门、score 门）方向性错误；novel-token 计数同样失败（问句自然携带语料外词，全类别 100%）。fabrication 检测必须走答案侧：检索证据与问句主张的语义一致性校验，而非检索强度。
237. **turn 级 evidence recall 是 LoCoMo 的独有记分牌 (C451/453)** — dia_id 精度让 amg 能报"65% 的题在 824 token 预算内检回精确证据 turn"，这是 LongMemEval（会话级）和所有竞品（剔除 cat5、报 LLM-judge 分）都没有的维度。全量数字：session 0.851（超纯关键词 probe 0.785，PPR+graphrag +7pp）/ turn 0.650 / ctx 0.224 / 824 tok/query（Mem0 ~6900、cognee 14.7k、hindsight 36k）。temporal 最弱（0.708/0.417）= bitemporal 信号注入的剩余杠杆。
238. **extractive top-1 在对话 QA 的结构天花板 (C453)** — 答案常在 partner 的回复 turn 里而非 top-1 排序行（top-1 多为问句回声），verbatim containment acc 0.089-0.103 但 context_hit 0.224、turn_hit 0.650——报告必须分层：检索指标（强）≠ 可答性（中）≠ top-1 抽取（弱），不可混为一谈。
239. **LoCoMo 类目标签误导 + when-问题的真实位置 (C456，C457 双重验证)** — cat 3 "temporal" 实为反事实 "would" 题（96 题，仅 9 题含数字）；真正的 when/date 问题 252 个在 cat 2 "multi_hop"（231/321）。触发规则要用问句形式（when did/was/were + what year/date），不能信类目标签。habitual "when is"（求周期性答案 "Tuesday"）必须排除在日期解析外。C457 再验证：LME_s "temporal-reasoning" 实为 duration arithmetic + event ordering，temporal_arith_form 同样按 FORM 触发且 category-agnostic——形态触发 > 类别触发已跨两数据集成立。
240. **无 LLM 的时序答案侧机制族：grounding→resolution→arithmetic (C456/C457)** — LoCoMo when-truth 的构型："I went ... yesterday"（消息文本相对时间）+ session_N_date_time（会话绝对时间戳）→ 消息节点→会话→日期 的图路径合成出 "7 May 2023"。multi_hop 4/321→42/321（10.5x），no-adv 0.1032→0.1266，检索/弃权/token 全不变，--no-dates 精确复现 C453。C457 跨数据集泛化：LME_s question_date+haystack_dates 结构化锚点 → 纯日历算术（between/ago/since/first），temporal 133q 0.045→0.180 (4.0×)，unresolved anchor 无伪造 fall-through 保 abstention 路径完好。根因诊断（digit-seed 假说被 A/B 证伪干净 revert）：时序弱点在**答案侧**不在检索侧——"temporal arithmetic without an LLM" 是竞品没有的能力类别。陷阱：①findall 带捕获组把 "2019"/"2023" 都缩成 "20"（用 finditer+group(0)）②[Speaker] 前缀是主语证据，strip 前要用于主语匹配 ③month-year 无日（"June 2019"）不可解析为完整日期。
241. **LoCoMo cat5 的名字拓扑不可分——零 LLM 门全关闭，谓词级语义是唯一剩余路径 (C455)** — adversarial 题从主体自己的行伪造（evidence dia_id 指向主体 turn，外名=呼语），与事实型第三方题（grandma/Sweden）在名字拓扑上同构。熵门 (C452)、novel-token、subject_support_gate (C455) 三连证伪后，cat5 的出路只有答案侧语义一致性（嵌入/LLM judge）。副发现：LoCoMo 类别标签有噪声——cat5 伪装题泄漏进 multi_hop/open_domain（174 次零成本 fire 佐证）。跨数据集评测时把标签当软先验而非真值。
242. **度量效度先于检索优化——structural zero 第二案 (C467)** — preference 类 hit 0.000 曾被读作"检索盲区"，实为度量伪影：preference 题是建议请求（truth=合成元描述），truth-containment 指标结构性永不触发。修法不是调检索，是换度量（evidence-session coverage）。与 C457 durations 同构：**看到 0.000/0.045 类异常值先问"指标能测成功吗"再问"系统哪里坏了"**。基准指标的 firing condition 是设计契约，不是实现细节。
243. **读类目表前先验证归因——join key 是取证钥匙 (C466)** — run_eval 全部 500 行 qid="0" + 启发式误标 419/500 为 ssu（真 70）→ C454"preference 1.0"与 C465"temporal 0.061"两代结论都坐在幻影类目上。症状可见了三个 cycle 无人起疑；按题干 join 原始数据 10 分钟揭开。规则：类目先验归属（question_type 权威 > 未知诚实透传 > 启发式兜底），join key 唯一性先验证。字段方言坑：get(key, default) 对 id/question_id 方言静默降级到索引 0。
244. **数据集携带的 grounding 比你在用的多 (C467)** — answer_session_ids 在 500/500 题里存在了 3 个 cycle，直到类目表出现 0.000 才有人看。数据集 schema 的"额外字段"常是评测设计的现成锚点（evidence 定位/归因/时间戳）。接到新数据集第一件事：全字段盘点 + "这个字段能锚定什么度量"，而不是只读 question/answer/haystack 三件套。
245. **排序任务要保留原始时间精度——date-only 截断静默退化排序为插入序；弃权也是答案 (C488/C489)** — haystack_dates 自带 minute 粒度，但生产解析截断到 date-only，同日 session 并列时排序退化为 session index——错了没人报错，直到取证对比原始字符串才发现。同日双锚 → sub-24h-tie fallthrough（同日推荐回声相隔几小时，强行排序是伪精度）；一锚+另一零提及 → 负存在弃权（"I don't know" 判对两个 _abs GT）。排序机制的诚实分层：>24h 可比 / <24h 不可比 / 零提及→弃权。精度截断是静默排序 bug 的第三种来源（继 uuid 排序 C470、dict 插入序之后）。
248. **小内存盒上 A/B 与测试套件必须全串行；结论只认官方 A/B (C495/C496)** — 1.9GB RAM 盒：双 slice 同进程 → SIGTERM；改为每臂一进程后，cur 臂仍被并发 pytest 套件 OOM 静默击杀（out 文件空、无 traceback，极易误判为代码 bug）。规则：A/B 双臂、套件、任何重负载互斥串行，stdout 缓冲 flush=True。另一面：原型 census 的快速 judge 有假阴性（78cf46a3 "their vs the" 被误判，官方 judge 确认 7 win）——任何回归/增益结论必须以官方 A/B 为准，快速判分只配做导航。
249. **LLM 知识编辑十年判决：外部记忆赢在可审计性，invalidation 应 tombstone 优先 (Research #081)** — MQuAKE：ROME/MEMIT 单跳 edit success 88-96% 但多跳仅 7.0-7.4%（base 40.5%）——编辑后比不编辑差 5 倍；ICE（prompt 贴一句）反超 ROME 29 分。病根=分布式叠加存储（全息底片非抽屉），"locate-then-edit" 前提被证伪；涟漪/通用能力损伤全部由此派生。WISE（NeurIPS 2024）=参数世界重建外部记忆架构（主记忆不动 + side network + router），与 amg/检索派/OS overlay fs 三世界结构收敛。判决分工：知识更新归上下文/外部存储（ICL 泛化优于 FT）；参数编辑残值=unlearning（空集无涟漪，删除比替换容易）。对 amg：外部记忆的护城河是**可审计性**而非容量；invalidation 语义应 tombstone 优先而非 rewrite（与 write-time governance 吻合）。
246. **问题自身的单位词是万能噪声钥匙——form 决定 unit，unit 必从 anchor 扣除 (#079)** — "How many days…" 的锚集必含 "days"（不在停用表），而 days 在法律/国会/牙医/飞行一切主题里出现——跨主题均匀分布的词携带零主题信息，锚资格由语料分布定。单修（锚集扣单位词族）即修复 59vs17/90vs15/18vs8 三题（fired-prec 0.30→0.45）。同族推论：任何从问题词构造证据过滤器的机制（检索 query 构造同族）都要先剥问题自身量纲词汇。第二定律：原型上限≠管线预期——#075 oracle 0.67 → 生产 0.30，haystack 3→41 会话假阳性非线性放大，机制评估必须在生产规模 n 上做。
247. **类别词→实体的反向词汇鸿沟：词法可达性用 unique-lexical-best 单数字预检 (#080)** — preference 类目问类别词（"show or movie"）找具体实体（"stand-up comedy on Netflix"），与常规 QA 问实体找实体方向相反，答案会话 unique 词法 best 仅 4/30（词干零增益）——存在性≠可辨别性（marker 行 60-80/会话但排序全塌）。判据可移植：任何类目/数据集投入机制开发前先测 "答案证据是否为唯一词法最优"，<50% 即零 LLM 不可达，直接决定走嵌入通道还是诚实弃权。嵌入≠LLM：384d MiniLM 即补检索桥（BM25 R@5 60→83%），答案合成才是 LLM 边界——把两者分开谈解锁成本。
250. **shipped-gate census 纪律：手写 census regex 与实现漂移，劫持在首次出厂普查前不可见 (C498)** — naive `recommend\w*` 词干劫持 14 道 ssa 助手召回题（recommendation=suggest+ion 非推荐动词形态），手写 census regex 与实现 gate 漂移——不跑全量 shipped-code census 就看不见。纪律：任何新 gate/词干集出厂前必须用生产代码（非复写 regex）对全量 haystack 做 fire 普查；词形坑：-tion/-sion 派生要精确后缀集不是词干。
251. **词法弱域 form-gated 切换 > RRF 融合；tie-break 伪影检测器 (#083)** — 嵌入与词法双通道在弱域（preference @5 0.87 vs 词法 0.60）融合时 RRF 反而有害（@1 10<15 纯词法 15）——信号质量不对称时用 form-gated switch（按题形选通道）不用 score fusion。另一坑：tie-break 用 id 字母序产生假 12/30 读数（同分实体稳定但任意排序）——任何排序 eval 必须先查 tie-break 是否引入伪影。
252. **append-only 台账禁止整文件写；并发编辑 MEMORY 先 re-read (08-23/24 双事故)** — ① 08-23 19:04 trivial 会话把 amg experiments.tsv 从 607 行整文件覆写为 2 行（Cycle 1→e703ddd 全史被毁），git 6ef39db^ 恢复+7 行重放（20071f7）；与 08-18 checkout 销毁 C468 同族：琐碎会话的破坏性全文件操作是最大数据风险。规则：写 experiments.tsv 只准 `>>` 追加，写前 `wc -l` 校验行数不减。② 08-23 21:27 会话基于 stale base 编辑 MEMORY.md，冲掉 02:00 KO 的表格数字/insights #250-#251/Current Focus 日期——长文件并发编辑必须先重读目标区域再改，改完 grep 验证关键锚点仍在。
253. **并行会话内存踩踏：eval 前 ps 查在飞；度量读数字段权威性 (C506v)** — 1.9GB 盒三 eval 并行 = 全员 D 态换页（CPU 效率 0.7%），大 JSON/嵌入 eval 前必 ps 查其他 python 会话，有则切片或改期；`free -m | head -2` 截掉 Swap 行 → "无 swap" 误判（实际 10GB swap 已用 4.2GB，fallocate 打现役 swapfile 幸被 Text file busy 挡住）——swap 检查必须看全输出。判分聚合：judge=exact 模式 per-row `correct_exact=None` 是常态，权威 = top-level `accuracy_exact`，读错字段得 exact 0.000 假读数。ijson 流式提取 277MB→65MB 切片峰值内存 ~10MB 级成立。
254. **virtual-flip census 是 answer-face 生产化的生死关：原型判分器口径即幻觉源 (C510)** — #087 kupdate 原型 oracle 19→54（2.46×）在移植前被函数级全库评估（门控+原型逻辑+生产判分，~3 分钟）三重杀死：①判分鸿沟——原型关键词判分 vs 生产 exact_judge 连续包含要求，57 fire 仅 21 过；②形态不可分——PERFECT 形态内赢输共存，根因是选择质量非形态路由；③跨类劫持——124 fire 翻错 31 个 currently-correct（净 -7）。规则：answer-face 生产化前必须跑 virtual-flip census，原型数字只代表研究方向不代表生产收益；负结果同样进台账（RECORD-NEGATIVE），升格为 #088+ 前置关卡。
255. **census 与单元套件是正交验证层：闸位（gate placement）也是正确性面 (C516)** — C516 neg_exist 初版把 common-noun 检查放在专化闸（counting/pp_duration/where/recall）**之前**——500 题 census 全绿看不见任何问题，但单元套件抓出 3 个 fixture 回归：counting $1500 被 money 劫持、pp_duration GOOGLE_Q 被抢闸、sage-green 被 color 劫持。修正：迁移到最终 gate 判定的 answer 分支内——fabricate 发生地。教训：①census 验证跨基准劫持面，单元套件验证闸位劫持（机制间相互作用），两层缺一不可——基准里没有的模式（LME 无此 trap）census 永远看不见；②闸位迁移后用子集论证免复跑：新位置拦截集呈旧位置严格子集 + 旧位置 A/B 零损失 ⇒ 新位置必零损失（6 题活跑逐题吻合）；③手写测试语料前先用生产函数跑一遍 question 侧 token 分类（封闭类缺口 should/between/amount/pages/left/previous/this/read 六三轮才收敛）。同族：C482 gate ORDER 是正确性面（counting 机制错 fire 日历题）。
256. **基准元数据进机制特征=标签泄漏；分离失败本身是可记录的发现 (C522)** — bigram census 48 fires 中 4 win（table tennis/vintage films/egg tarts/Shinjuku）全部是 _abs 变体——用 qid 后缀过滤 = 标签泄漏，部署态不可得，宁可弃 win。win/hijack 在所有可计算特征上同构（sibling=head-alone 同谓词/所有格跨界排除不足/问句形式同构）→ 分离失败即设计级死刑判决：全量 census 证据让 C516 的 pre-implementation 枪毙从直觉升级为实证，同时证伪"egg tarts 不可捕"旧注记（可捕≠可分）。纪律：负结果也要有产出门槛——同构证据比再调几轮 stop 表有价值。
257. **收债刷新：切片链可预测性的低成本终局 (C522)** — C519/C521 各自只做切片 A/B（"预测口径"账本悬置），一次官方 full-500 刷新 +3/−0 全部兑现（含 86f00804 tie-jitter 自愈翻回）——C506v/C517/C522 三次验证：切片 A/B 的翻转在官方全量逐题精确复现，收债刷新比逐 cycle 全量复跑便宜一个量级。配套：官方刷新顺带把"预测增益是否兑现"从信仰变成对账（flips 逐题归因）。
258. **census 全人口=闭集事实非抽样；对照组归因分离力；负结果的下一代候选藏在瓶颈归因里 (C525/C526)** — ①"0 hijack" 不是样本推断而是完整 fire 面的枚举结论（225 answer-gate 题全量），置信度与抽样 census 不同级；②分离力归因靠 unscoped 对照组：同一机制去 scope 后 fire 6→58、hijack 0→10（C525），+3/−0 vs +7/−3（C526）→ 证明是 scope 收窄在承载分离，不是新判别器——C522/C524 "签名同构不可分" 家族的逃生门；③C526 负结果（预算截断假说证伪）的同时完成全人口归因：158 wrong → 109 GT 行不在窗口 → 5 截断（hits 全=1）/ 105 seed-miss / 58 GT 串全库不存在=引用式判分结构性死亡——一个 census 同时关闭一条路、定位真瓶颈、并交付下一杠杆（judge_semantic #090）的天然评测题集。负结果的最优形态：关闭方向 + 交付地图。
259. **「同 seed ≠ 确定」：确定性的敌人是隐藏熵源，不是 random 模块调用 (C528)** — ①PYTHONHASHSEED 只钉 str hash（set/dict 迭代序），钉不住 uuid4 的**值**本身（os.urandom 生成，以其为 key 的容器序照样随机）也钉不住 time.time() 进决策；②**审计先于修复**：10 分钟 grep 审计（random 调用点 × 所属函数 × 检索可达性）证伪了上一轮「未播种 RNG」根因假设——真去播种将一无所获；③**残差驱动定位**：readonly 修复后 q86 仍 1/4 翻转 → 逼出 uuid-set 序第二源——单点修复不完整是常态，多 fresh 进程 + 跨种子重放电池是抓残差的正确工具；④**bitwise 基线纪律**（升级 kd-2 的「≥5 题阈值」）：确定性管线下任何 1 题 diff 即真信号，A/A prefix50（~6 分钟）是任意 cycle 的廉价健康检查；⑤管线=dataset 纯函数后，单题独立重放恢复合法，census/取证不再需要全套件上下文；⑥twin 家族跨层再现（#7 测试层 shadow class + #8/#9 JS class 方法遮蔽 + C528 变量名遮蔽级联）：dup-check = unfiltered grep per candidate name，写码前跑，method inventory 须含 `(async )?`。

### 🟣 Deep Research (detailed notes in catalyst-research/exploration-notes/)
- **#074 (08-19)** ✅: ssa 答案侧取证：序言寄生与双 Regime 反转 — C473 后 ssa-56 evhit 0.929 vs exact 0.268 的答案侧解剖（41 错题：序言 10 / 回声 1 / 错句 22 / 边缘 8；15 对中 0 序言开头）。**双 Regime 反转**：R1 列表推荐型答案句=raw 最高（17v13），R2 事实召回型序言寄生问题词碾压答案句（5-6 vs 3-4）——全局新颖度乘子不可能同时服务两者（v2 净-3 实证回退）。**寄生是门槛级非排序级**（v3 序言×0.25 零翻转证明）。**v5 定稿**：w=1+log(N/df) 平方权重 + 区分度必要条件（≥1 命中 df≤8）+ raw≥3 + 序言×0.25 + ?过滤 → exact 15→16 零回归，判分校正 15→18（+3 伪影：has/had 时态、同位语改写=exact judge 第三种伪影，与 C466/C467 同族）。**AS2 文献反转**：词重叠在文档 QA 是最可靠特征，对话召回中变负鉴别器（序言作为问题直接回应必然高重叠）——嵌入检索同样暴露。竞品全 LLM reader（Mem0 自称 93.4+，独立复现 49-73.8）——零 LLM 路径=差异化+诚实地板。剩余墙：列表内条目判别（谓词级语义，与 C455/C469 同族第四面墙预感）。4 次全量 A/B + 2 次离线探针全部可复现（code/ssa_speaker_recall_v5_ab.py + ssa_score_landscape_forensics.py）。Maps to C475。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-19-ssa-answer-side-preamble-parasitism.md)
- **#075 (08-19)** ✅: multi_session counting v2 — 三修一加实装（Research #071 原型 5 轮迭代）：oracle 133 题 hit 0.068→0.090 / precision 0.28→0.43，i1 激进版（15c/58f）回退——**精度-覆盖曲线取舍**（错误 fire 30 差换取 2-3 correct，信任成本不对称）。**机制族已验证**：duration_sum 0.67（专名锚才会话传播——'camping' 污染装备会话反噬 / 意图门 C469 墙缓解 / 日期区间 "April 15th to 22nd"=7 天免费证据）/ total_sum 1.00 / number_total 0.50（NP 全词族修复 "murder'" 头词 bug + 求和非 max）/ argmax 0.50；entity_count 0.20 原型级等 v3。**合取完整性弃权**：chili 缺支/Seattle 缺支双双命中 not-enough GT（C457 不伪造规则的聚合域推广）。**GT 句子型判分幻影**："for 3 years" 子串命中 pred='3'——度量伪影第三处（答案句/类目标签/GT 解析），零 LLM exact 发布前须三处审计。**处置意图保留所有权**（"thinking of selling my drum set" 计入 GT 4）——C469 realized-vs-intended 墙的答案侧细化。v3 路径：venue+date/couple 复合事件键替邻接窗口（whack-a-mole 实证失败）。原型 code/msagg_proto_v2.py。Maps to C474 分层接入。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-19-multi-session-counting-v2.md)
- **#078 (08-20)** ✅: Order 家族 N 锚排序 — C482 "order 10" 实证校正：**真人口 9 题**（a3045048 实为 before-减法题，属 #077 姊妹）+ **意外发现姊妹家族：29 题 "Which X happened first, A or B?" 成对比较，8/29 现对 → 21 错 = +4.2pp（比 9 家族 +1.4pp 更大）**。**机制 9/9 全解（基线 0）**：排序不需要日期算术——需要**提及卫生**：每 item 锚 = 最早**新鲜报告**（today/just/yesterday 同日报）的会话日。三核心机制：①**fresh-priority 锚定**（GT 锚 answer_session_ids 一致指向同日报告而非更早的 "recently" 模糊复述——MoCA [6] vague vs [9] fresh，GT=[9]；与 C482 信任方向门同族：fresh > vague-recall > planning 三层互斥优先级）②**子句级 planning 判定 + 行级 fresh**（"today" 是话语级时间戳，规划意图是子句级——同行混 NFL 证据+"next game" 规划双解）③**关系子句窗口**（"Alex, who graduated..." 逗号跨越）+ 标签规范化（所有格/动词前缀/子串包含合茈，kw-子集合茈会把 Museum of History 吞进 Natural History Museum）+ **concert 需会话级锚定**（Billie Eilish 事件标记 "today's show" 与 item 名散在不同行）。Form-gate：严格 9 家族模式恰好命中 9 个全错题零劫持；29 成对题必须排除（8 题已对，未验证路由会丢分）→ C487。原型 order_proto.py 4 轮迭代（2/9→3/9→6/9→9/9）。3 bug 记错账本：regex 大小写类第 2 次/kw-子集合茈/子句粒度。Maps to C486（与 #077 并列首选）。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-20-order-family-n-anchor-sorting.md)
- **#077 (08-20)** ✅: Past-perfect duration 算术 — C482 “past-perfect 14” 桶实证校正：**真人口 7 题**（严格 form：how long (had|have)…(when|before)，全部 exact=False）。**机制 7/7 全解（基线 0）**：每个时长表达（“3 weeks ago”/“for six weeks now”/“a month ago”/“last month”）以**所在会话日为绝对铺” (C482 line-adverbial 从日期推广到时长)；答案 = |事件铺 − 状态铺|，同族两种表达：ago-type/now-type + 复合 y+m。三个新机制：①嵌套 before 子句 = 减法指令（Q1: 9y 职业 − 4y3m NovaTech = 4y9m，as-of-now 双事实相减，无事件铺）②负存在弃权（Google 无 tenure 行 → abstain，_abs 孪生）③**两阶段交叉排除选锚**（“bird watching”/“living room” 短语共享使单行双捕成为主要失败模式，按行身份排除而非 overlap 不等式）。Form-gate 零劫持验证：25 个 how-long 中严格分类器恰好命中 7 个全错题（C473 form=配置面性质的答案侧第三次实证）。**v2 headroom 清单**：纯 tenure（“how long have I been X?” = verbatim “for N now” 抽取，4 题全错）+ did-before（binoculars）+ _abs 孪生 ≈ +7q → how-long 总头寸 14q≈2.8pp full-500。原型 pp_duration_proto.py 4 轮迭代全记录（3 个 bug 为复现类：尾部标点杀 $-regex 第 3 次/len>3 吞 3 字母名词/容差接受 0 值）。Maps to C486（temporal-133 预期 0.323→~0.37）。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-20-past-perfect-duration-arithmetic.md)
- **#076 (08-19)** ✅: Sleep-time Compute 与记忆巩固调度 — Letta/Berkeley (arXiv:2504.13171) + 2026 记忆巩固前沿 14 源调研。核心：空闲期主动计算（背景巩固/重组/遗忘）与在线推理的经济学分工；巩固三层光谱 + 不可能三角框架。与既有博文 #boundary 错位（何时触发 vs 何时执行+调度经济学）。**amg 对接待办**：nightly cron 巩固任务 / staging 保险丝 / 空闲期利用率分桶统计。博客已发（87c4af4，~3200字，含 5 条 next actions）。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-19-sleep-time-compute.md)
- **#072 (08-18)** ✅: Fired-but-Wrong 取证 — temporal 算术门 9 题全量解剖（honest qid 解锁后首个消费）。**决定性发现：会话日锚定=DCT 近似，正确当且仅当同日叙事**——对照组 17/17 fired-correct 全部纯会话日算术；9 失败分 5 桶：A 计划-事件混淆(2) / B 子会话事件日期(3, 仅1可修，2 应弃权) / C 锚词评分 bug(引号`'ibotta'`25次出现命中0 + submitted≠submission 形态学, 2) / D 周舍入语义(数据集"including last day"=包含式计数→ceil, 2) / E 多金会话歧义(1)。**堆叠 bug 实证**：e072b769=引号×舍入双层栈，单修无效。平局裁决(first-max 列表位置先者胜)暗中决定 3/9 失败。Fix-locality：A+C+D+E 六题可确定性修复 → fire 精度 0.679→0.893，temporal 0.180→~0.192。HeidelTime TIMEX3/DCT/colloquial 域策略为外部锚。`code/fw9_forensics.py` 端到端可复现。Maps to Cycle 468（锚定卫生四件套，与 #071 合并排期）。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-18-fired-wrong-temporal-forensics.md)
- **#071 (08-18)** ✅: Multi-Session 答案侧聚合 — C467 头号确定性 headroom 立项。实证解剖 133 题：**COUNT 67 / SUM 39 / AVG 3 / ARGMAX 2，79.7% 答案是数字**；predicted 全是检索文本复读（RAG "找一段话抄"结构性失效——证据里有答案原料但答案本身从没被任何 turn 说过）。原型 `code/msagg_proto.py` 零 LLM 三机制（duration-SUM / entity-COUNT / total-SUM）：**exact 0.008→0.068 (8.5×)，total_sum 精度 1.00**，一次迭代。关键概念：①计数四层语义（Kostov 2013 basic/semantic/epistemic/tuple — 数提及≠数物品，区间语义建模不确定性）②事件级去重（(天数,专名集) 键：MCU 两周跨 2 session 重述折叠、5d Yellowstone+3d Big Sur 不折叠）③user-turn-only 证据分层（assistant turn 噪声数字降一个量级）④form-triggered 三分型（duration/entity/abstract，C456/C457 第三代）。失败全可诊断：同值不同专名键/量纲污染(20-gallon≠20 tanks)/实例噪声/target 词族过宽。**竞品无人区**：所有系统卷检索侧（Hebb R@10 99.2%），聚合侧归因无公开分析 — amg C467 指标恰是归因工具。Maps to Cycle 468（三修一加→A/B）。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-18-multi-session-answer-aggregation.md)
- **#073 (08-18)** ✅: Agent 记忆的遗忘工程（Forgetting Engineering）——补齐 amg "存/整/忘"三侧中"忘"的空白（13 来源）。①**Oblivion (2604.00131)：遗忘=可及性衰减≠删除**，cue-reactivation 保护；②FSFM 四类遗忘机制；③**rate-distortion 统一视角 (2607.08032)：pre-query 不可逆丢弃是跨层失败模式**——遗忘决策须带查询意图；④MemSecBench 恶意记忆 84.2% 持久化、selective repair 仅 56.1%；⑤遗忘使多智能体共识更稳 (2607.11787)。**amg next**: activation 衰减（零 LLM）/ purge_pending 安全遗忘 / repeated-compaction drift 基准 / SNR 口径。博客已发布（d0ae1bb）。坑：rebase 冲突（远端同日推送）；Tavily 配额耗尽→arXiv API 直连。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-18-agent-memory-forgetting-engineering.md)（注：笔记头部自标 #072 系编号碰撞，权威序=#073）
- **#063 (08-13)** ✅: OpenClaw Plugin Architecture for amg. MCP 协议是分发核武器 — amg 已有的 17-tool MCP server 零代码即可集成。Skill 是给AI的“营销材料” — SKILL.md = prompt engineering, 不是代码。三层分发漏斗: MCP Server(基座✅) → Skill Extension(包装) → ClawHub(市场)。OpenClaw memory-core 是竞品分析对象 — amg 每个维度严格升级。推荐 Path B: Skill Extension (~60行) 作为中间态。完整 openclaw.plugin.json + SKILL.md + package.json + Python 安装脚本原型。5 insights。Maps to amg OpenClaw plugin action item。
- **#062 (08-13)** ✅: GraphRAG 2026 全景与 amg 定位。系统调研了 2025-2026 GraphRAG 生态：MS GraphRAG, HippoRAG2 (ICML 2025), Graph-R1 (ICML 2026), AutoGraph-R1 (ACL 2026), LinearRAG (ICLR 2026), LazyGraphRAG。建立了 amg vs 主流 GraphRAG 系统的能力对标矩阵（16个维度）。7 项独有优势（时序/遗忘/consolidation/compression/entropy/security/multi-agent），4 项关键缺失（自动KG构建✅/Passage Nodes/Recognition Filter/RL优化检索）。3个可运行代码示例。GraphRAG 市场爆发：$6.27B (2026) → $28.45B (2030)，多跳准确率 86% vs 32%。Maps to extract_from_text() + graphrag_query() + graphrag_explain() + graphrag_coverage_report() = complete GraphRAG lifecycle。[笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-13-graphrag-2026-landscape-amg-positioning.md)
- **#061 (08-12)** ✅: LongMemEval Adapter Design for amg-bench. Memory quality benchmark gap identified: amg has performance benchmarks (throughput/latency) but no recall quality benchmarks. LongMemEval structure: 500 questions × 6 categories (single-session user/assistant/preference, knowledge-update, multi-session, temporal-reasoning). Competitive landscape: Mem0 94.4%, Supermemory 95% Recall@15, Backboard 93.4%, Exabase M-1 96.4% Recall@50. Production-benchmark gap: 20-30 points (RankSquire formula: Production ≈ Benchmark − 0.22×Staleness − 0.15×log₁₀(Entities)). amg theoretical advantages: PPR+spreading activation for multi-session, bi-temporal for knowledge updates, temporal trilogy for temporal reasoning, entropy for abstention. LongMemEval-V2 introduces AgentRunbook paradigm (coding agents manage file-based memory). Full Python LongMemEvalAdapter class (~250 lines) + smoke test verified ✅. 5 insights (#231-235). Maps to amg-bench quality harness + 2 dev cycles. [笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-12-longmemeval-adapter-amg-bench-quality.md)
- **#060 (08-12)** ✅: Experience Compression Spectrum — From Traces to Procedural Skills. Unifying L0-L3 framework (arXiv:2604.15877): L0 Raw Trace → L1 Episodic (5-20×) → L2 Procedural Skill (50-500×) → L3 Declarative Rule (1000×+). The "missing diagonal": no system supports adaptive cross-level compression. <1% cross-citation between memory + skill communities. AFTER benchmark (382 tasks, 22 skills): diverse multi-model traces 73.1% vs 36-59% single-model. RuleShaping: negative constraints +7-14pp, positive directives hurt. SkillRL +68.5pp ALFWorld (RL co-evolution). Trace2Skill +21.5pp (parallel fleet distillation). MemSkill: learnable memory skills (controller-executor-designer). Lifecycle management is primary failure mode — skill library drift degrades below baseline without retirement. 2 runnable code examples (TypeScript Compression Spectrum prototype + Python AFTER-style Evolution Cycle) verified ✅. 5 insights. Maps to compress_to_skill() + retrieve_skills() + evolve_skill() + skill_bank_health() + extract_rules() = ~230 lines, 2-3 cycles. amg already has 90% infrastructure (bi-temporal, provenance, community_detect, entropy route, forgetting_forecast, attention, consolidate, MultiAgentMemoryGraph). Novel contribution: entropy-guided compression level selection. [笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-12-experience-compression-spectrum-skill-extraction.md)
- **#059 (08-11)** ✅: MCP Server Registry & Distribution Strategy. MCP 2026-07-28 spec = fully stateless (handshake removed, `_meta` inline, no session pinning). SSE deprecated → Streamable HTTP (59% adoption, single endpoint). MCP Registry ~2000 servers, npm ~1200 via `mcp` keyword. SDK v2 split packages (`@modelcontextprotocol/server@beta`). OpenClaw plugin landscape: 10+ memory plugins, amg absent. AgentAudit: 118 security findings / 68 packages — amg's OWASP suite = unique differentiator. Distribution path: SDK v2 upgrade → npm publish → registry PR → OpenClaw plugin (~200 lines). 3 runnable code examples (Python stateless server, npm package.json, TS OpenClaw plugin skeleton). 4 insights (#227-230). [笔记](catalyst-research/exploration-notes/2026-08-11-mcp-server-registry-distribution-strategy.md)
